Um, well... I want to assure you that I am still trying to do those things. I really don't want to sound ungrateful for my hobbies or like I'm just sitting around doing nothing, because I promise I'm still trying to stay engaged. 

But... I guess, if I'm being totally honest, it feels a little different lately. Most days, I kind of wake up with this... this flat, heavy sadness. And usually, you know, my hobbies would fix that. Putting on a record, cooking a nice meal, or just tinkering with some old electronics—those used to be the things that would completely reset me after a long day. 

But recently... it's like they just don't reach me anymore? I'll be holding a soldering iron or chopping vegetables, and I just feel sort of empty or disconnected from it. I feel terrible even complaining about it, because I know I'm lucky to have things I enjoy, and I really am trying to keep at them, just hoping the spark comes back. 

Does... does that answer your question? I hope I'm explaining it right, I just don't want you to think I've given up on the things I love.
